Our 150 prompts — same guy in every video

The guy (every video): white, about 30, six feet, strong muscular build (broad shoulders, big arms, veins on the forearms), long dark-blond hair in a loose low ponytail, short full beard. Outfit A (2 of every 3 videos): plain black sleeveless tank top, khaki work shorts, tan boots, brown gloves. Outfit B (every 3rd): skintight charcoal compression tee, fitted black work joggers. Nothing printed on his clothes (H3 garbles printed words).

Every video: 15.08 s (362 frames, the longest length our H3 model was trained for), ONE locked high-wide camera, US suburban back yard, starts on plain lawn, build happens as one sped-up time-lapse take, real yard sounds only, ends with him walking into the foreground side-on and looking at the finished build.

How he stays the same guy: video #1 renders from text. We pick the best frame of him from it, and every video after that uses that frame as his reference picture (H3's reference mode, proven on 14 Aug to keep the same face across clips). That is why each prompt comes in two forms: T2VA (text only, used for #1) and Ref2VA (with his picture, used from #2 on).

Follow CTA: added after the render, never by H3. Our own animated "Follow → Following" button (cursor taps it, colour flip, sparkles) laid over the last 3 s by one ffmpeg step. It does not change the length (still 15.08 s).

Phase 1 — the proven builds (1–30)

#1 · Pool · outfit A · Built a Round Cinder Block Pool with Cedar Cladding from Scratch! 😱🏊‍♂️

Built a Round Cinder Block Pool with Cedar Cladding from Scratch! 😱🏊‍♂️ #diypool #backyardpool #cinderblock #yardtransformation #diyprojects

T2VA .txt

subject_definitions:
<Subject 1> is the man in <Picture 1>: a white man of about thirty, six feet tall, with a strong athletic muscular build: broad rounded shoulders, big arms with defined biceps and veins along the forearms, a solid chest and a flat stomach. He has long dark-blond hair past his shoulders, pulled back into a loose low ponytail with a few strands falling by his temples, a short full beard, light tanned skin and light eyes. He is the only person in the video and must look exactly like <Picture 1> in face, hair, beard and build.

summary:
[reference generation] One locked high-wide camera watches <Subject 1> build a pool project in a US suburban back yard as a single continuous time-lapse take, ending with him standing side-on in the foreground looking at the finished work.

retention_analysis:
<Subject 1> (appears in [Shot 1]): fully_preserved - face, long dark-blond ponytail, short full beard, skin tone, height and muscular build are retained; the clothing is the outfit written in the description, not the clothing in <Picture 1>.

detailed_description:
The target video is realistic smartphone-style live action with natural daylight, no colour grade and no music.
[Shot 1] Live-action, realistic, one continuous time-lapse take in bright clear morning sun with long soft shadows. One locked camera that never moves, high and wide, as if from an upstairs window about four metres up and eight metres back, looking down at the yard at an angle. The whole build area, the lawn, the fence and the back of the house are in the picture, with open sky at the top.
00:00.000-00:02.000: The back yard of a two-storey red-brick house with white windows, a tall cedar privacy fence on three sides and a big maple tree in the corner. The ground where the work happens is a flat, freshly mown green lawn with a rectangle of string lines and four wooden stakes marking where the pool goes. In the middle of the yard stands <Subject 1>, the long-haired muscular man from <Picture 1>. He wears a plain fitted black sleeveless tank top with deep armholes that leaves his whole shoulders and arms bare, khaki canvas work shorts ending just above the knee, tan leather work boots with grey socks, and brown leather work gloves. Nothing is printed on his clothes. He starts the job, working fast and sure.
00:02.000-00:10.500: Time-lapse. The whole build happens in one sped-up take from the same fixed camera: he moves around the work in quick jumps, the shadows of the fence and trees slide across the grass, and the work grows stage by stage. He lays the first ring of grey cinder blocks on a bed of sand, tapping each one level with a rubber mallet, and the outline of a round pool about four metres across appears on the grass. Course after course the grey block wall climbs to waist height, the cores filled with wet concrete from a wheelbarrow, until the whole block wall stands complete on the lawn. He unrolls a bright blue vinyl liner, drapes it over the wall and smooths it into the basin, clipping the top edge over the blocks with a long rail. He screws vertical cedar boards to the outside of the block wall board by board until no grey block shows, then sets a three-step wooden stair against one side.
00:10.500-00:12.500: Back to normal speed. A green garden hose lies over the rim and clear water swirls into the blue basin, the level rising to just below the top rail.
00:12.500-00:15.083: He walks a few steps toward the camera into the lower part of the picture and stops side-on to us, close enough that his arms, shoulders and build are clear. He pulls off one glove, wipes his forearm across his forehead, rests his hands on his hips and looks over the finished build. He stays there to the last frame. He is the only person in the yard the whole time, and there is only one of him. No lettering, signs, logos or house numbers anywhere.

overall_soundscape:
Real yard sound only: the scrape and thud of cinder blocks being set down, a rubber mallet tapping, a cordless drill in short bursts, a shovel in gravel, birds in the trees, a light breeze, a dog barking far away, and at the end running water or quiet. No talking.

non_diegetic_music:
N/A

He screws grey render to the outside of the block wall board by board until no grey block shows, then sets a border of white river stones.
00:10.500-00:12.500: Back to normal speed. A green garden hose lies over the rim and clear water swirls into the blue basin, the level rising to just below the top rail.
00:12.500-00:15.083: He walks a few steps toward the camera into the lower part of the picture and stops side-on to us, close enough that his arms, shoulders and build are clear. He pulls off one glove, wipes his forearm across his forehead, rests his hands on his hips and looks over the finished build. He stays there to the last frame. He is the only person in the yard the whole time, and there is only one of him. No lettering, signs, logos or house numbers anywhere.
